# 🔡 The character model as pure links (2,622 states)

A character-level MLP (3-character context, 92K parameters, trained on public README text) compiled into 2,622 Markdown states, one line each, with a link per likely next character. No Action, no JavaScript: **start at [`t` `h` `e`](#s-the)** and keep track of the text yourself. This used to be the tail of the main README; it lives in its own page so the README stays light. [Back to README-LM](../../README.md).
